| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:98 | - **Default.** Dividends, interest and gains on securities are taxed under the PFU unless the taxpayer elects the progressive scale ("Si vous ne spécifiez pas votre choix, vos revenus sont soumis au PFU"). The income tax part is in the F2613 table above; the social levies are added at the rate Section 2 gives for that income and that year. | Current LF76 / PFU-or-scale, What-the-scale-brings and Rates rows: global2OP, dated2025irrevocable/2026reversible,40%,6.8, RFRN-2/30November,2DC/2TR and regulated savings. Earlier scale bands route to fr-personal-income-tax rather than reprinting them here. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:99 | - **The election is global, express and irrevocable for the year.** It covers ALL investment income and ALL gains on securities of the household for that year ("L'option s'applique à l'ensemble de vos revenus mobiliers et de vos plus-values de cessions de valeurs mobilières"). It is made by ticking box 2OP on the return. The election for crypto gains is a separate one (box 3CN): see `fr-crypto-tax`. For income of 2026, declared in 2027, and later years, F2613 says the option can be withdrawn within the claim period or during an audit if it turns out worse ("vous pourrez renoncer à votre option pour le barème progressif (dans le délai de réclamation ou en cours de contrôle)"). For income of 2025 it stays irrevocable. | Current LF76 / PFU-or-scale, What-the-scale-brings and Rates rows: global2OP, dated2025irrevocable/2026reversible,40%,6.8, RFRN-2/30November,2DC/2TR and regulated savings. Earlier scale bands route to fr-personal-income-tax rather than reprinting them here. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:100 | - **What the election brings.** Under the progressive scale only: the dividend allowance in the F2613 table, the deductible part of the CSG in the F2329 table (deducted from income of the year the CSG is paid), and the deduction of fees and charges. Under the PFU, none of these. | Current LF76 / PFU-or-scale, What-the-scale-brings and Rates rows: global2OP, dated2025irrevocable/2026reversible,40%,6.8, RFRN-2/30November,2DC/2TR and regulated savings. Earlier scale bands route to fr-personal-income-tax rather than reprinting them here. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:101 | - **The rule of thumb on the page.** The F2613 table gives the page's own guidance by marginal rate (TMI). It compares rates only. With dividends in the third band or above the PFU is usually still better, but the dividend allowance and the deductible CSG move the answer, so compute both before electing. | Current LF76 / PFU-or-scale, What-the-scale-brings and Rates rows: global2OP, dated2025irrevocable/2026reversible,40%,6.8, RFRN-2/30November,2DC/2TR and regulated savings. Earlier scale bands route to fr-personal-income-tax rather than reprinting them here. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:102 | - **The advance payment.** When dividends and interest are paid, the bank withholds the income tax part of the PFU as an advance (prélèvement forfaitaire non libératoire), credited against the final tax on the return. A household whose reference income of the year before last is under the limit in the F2613 table for that income type (one pair of limits for dividends, a lower pair for interest) may ask the bank to be excused by 30 November of the year before payment. | Current LF76 / PFU-or-scale, What-the-scale-brings and Rates rows: global2OP, dated2025irrevocable/2026reversible,40%,6.8, RFRN-2/30November,2DC/2TR and regulated savings. Earlier scale bands route to fr-personal-income-tax rather than reprinting them here. |

| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:170 | - **Withdrawal before the end of the 5th year.** In principle any withdrawal closes the plan, with listed exceptions (F2385). | 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:171 | - **After the 5th year.** Gains are exempt from income tax; the social levies are due on the gain at each withdrawal ("Quelle que soit la date des retraits, les gains réalisés sont soumis aux prélèvements sociaux"). A PEA gain is investment income (F2329 lists "Gain réalisé ... en cas de retrait ou de clôture d'un PEA" under revenus de placements), so a withdrawal in 2026 bears the 2026 rate in the F2329 table, except where special CSG rules for gains or payments from before 2018 apply. | 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:197 | - **Premiums paid before 27 September 2017.** The rates in the brochure table are a final withholding that applies only if the taxpayer elects it when the gain is paid; otherwise the gain goes to the progressive scale (F22414: "Soit, au moment de leur versement, au prélèvement forfaitaire libératoire de 15 %, si vous choisissez cette option globale au moment du versement Soit, au moment du traitement de votre déclaration de revenus, au barème progressif"). After 8 years the yearly allowance in the F22414 table applies to these gains too. | Current LF80 and rate rows: premiumsdate/contractage/allcontracts/yearly allowance/IFU/150000split/electedoldrates. | 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:198 | - **Exempt withdrawals.** The gain is exempt from income tax, not from the social levies, where the withdrawal follows the taxpayer's or spouse's dismissal, retirement, invalidity or judicial liquidation of a non-salaried activity (F22414: "exonérés d'impôt sur le revenu si le rachat résulte (pour vous ou votre conjoint marié ou pacsé) de l'une des raisons suivantes : Licenciement Mise à la retraite Invalidité Liquidation judiciaire"). | Current LF80 and rate rows: premiumsdate/contractage/allcontracts/yearly allowance/IFU/150000split/electedoldrates. | 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:200 | - **Any withdrawal is taxable**, total or partial (F22414: "vous êtes imposé si vous effectuez un rachat (qu'il soit total ou partiel)"). Only the gain inside the amount withdrawn is taxed, not the premiums paid back. | Current LF80 and rate rows: premiumsdate/contractage/allcontracts/yearly allowance/IFU/150000split/electedoldrates. FR-A05 corrects old generic retirement/invalidity exemption: causation, early retirement, categories2–3, eligibleperson, deadline and invalidityPSexception. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:201 | - **The gain in each withdrawal.** The insurer computes the taxable gain contained in each withdrawal and reports it on the IFU form. Use that figure, and ask the insurer how it split the withdrawal between premiums and gains; do not treat a partial withdrawal as premiums only. | Current LF80 and rate rows: premiumsdate/contractage/allcontracts/yearly allowance/IFU/150000split/electedoldrates. | 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:246 | - **Shares subscribed from 1 January 2025.** Two gains. The exercise gain (value of the share on the day of exercise minus the price fixed at grant) is taxed at the flat rate in the table, or on option under the salary scale if the holder has 3 years or more; under 3 years the higher rate applies with no option. The sale gain (sale price minus value at exercise) is taxed under the PFU whatever the length of service, or under the scale on the global option, and the retiring director's allowance can apply. | Current LF84 and dated rows: exercise/sale split from2025,3yearservice,19/30/12.8/18.6, fixedallowance gate, issuer eligibility referral. Global scale option remains generalPFU alternative. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:247 | - **Shares subscribed before 1 January 2025.** One gain on sale, taxed as a gain on securities: BSPCE granted from 1 January 2018 with 3 years or more of service go under the PFU or the scale; under 3 years, the higher rate in the table with no option and no fixed allowance; BSPCE granted until 31 December 2017 use the two rates the table gives by length of service, with no holding allowance and no fixed allowance. | Current LF84 and dated rows: exercise/sale split from2025,3yearservice,19/30/12.8/18.6, fixedallowance gate, issuer eligibility referral. Global scale option remains generalPFU alternative. |

Global scale option remains generalPFU alternative. |

| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:324 | - **Main home.** The gain on selling the main home (residence principale) and its outbuildings is fully exempt (F10864). It is the home occupied for most of the year, and it must still be the main home at the time of the sale (F10864: "Le logement doit être votre résidence principale au moment de la vente"). | Current LF90 / Rates duration/cost/15000/surtax table and original property example. Both tax bases, notary2048and2042report,mainhome,former-resident150000 conditions preserved; furnished depreciation check separately routed. Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:325 | - **Two different allowances.** The holding allowance runs on two scales: one for income tax and a slower one for the social levies. The income tax rate in the table applies to the gain after the income tax allowance; the social levies (the lower total in the F2329 table, unchanged in 2026) apply to the gain after the social levy allowance. | Current LF90 / Rates duration/cost/15000/surtax table and original property example. Both tax bases, notary2048and2042report,mainhome,former-resident150000 conditions preserved; furnished depreciation check separately routed. Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:326 | - **The surtax** is due where the taxable gain, after the holding allowance, is MORE than the threshold in the BOFiP table. Its rate runs between the two ends in the F10864 table, from the table on form 2048-IMM-SD, and the notary computes it. It does not apply to exempt sales or to sales of building land. | Current LF90 / Rates duration/cost/15000/surtax table and original property example. Both tax bases, notary2048and2042report,mainhome,former-resident150000 conditions preserved; furnished depreciation check separately routed. Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |
| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:327 | - **The notary files and pays.** The notary computes the taxable gain and the tax, files the return and pays the tax at the sale (F10864: "Calcul de la plus-value imposable et du montant de l'impôt à payer"); the seller then indicates on the income tax return the gain the notary declared (F10864: "Montant de la plus-value déclarée par le notaire"). | Current LF90 / Rates duration/cost/15000/surtax table and original property example. Both tax bases, notary2048and2042report,mainhome,former-resident150000 conditions preserved; furnished depreciation check separately routed. Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |

| 15e343e3ae03dd5d22f9f8af9aedfdde98492302:367 | 1. **Sort every item by type and year.** List each dividend, interest payment, disposal of securities, PEA or life insurance withdrawal, employee share sale and property sale, with the date received or sold. Forms/IFU are retained in asset-specific paragraphs and intake. |

Net the year's losses on securities against the year's gains first. | Current corresponding taxable-base paragraph plus Deadlines supplies same workflow; no mandatory planning order fabricated. Forms/IFU are retained in asset-specific paragraphs and intake. |

Run the household computation in `fr-personal-income-tax`. Forms/IFU are retained in asset-specific paragraphs and intake. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:18 | The return filed in 2026 covers income received and disposals made in 2025. Income and disposals of 2026 are declared in 2027. Where the two years differ, this skill names the year. Crypto gains are in `fr-crypto-tax`, rental income in `fr-rental-income`, the progressive scale and the household computation in `fr-personal-income-tax`, the CSG and CRDS in `fr-social-contributions`, non-residents in `fr-cross-border`, and the exit tax in `leaving-france-exit-tax`. | Exact retained successor LF20, France: capital gains, investment income and employee shares |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:22 | - **In scope:** individuals who are French tax residents (CGI art. 4 B), taxed on dividends, interest, gains on securities (CGI art. 150-0 A ff.), the PEA, life insurance withdrawals, employee shares, employee savings, PER exits, and gains on property held privately (CGI art. 150 U ff.). | Exact retained successor LF24, Who this applies to |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:23 | - **Social levies** (CSG, CRDS, solidarity levy) apply on top of income tax to residents (CSS L. 136-6, L. 136-7). A resident insured in another EEA state or Switzerland pays only the 7.5% solidarity levy (F2329). | FR-A08 current foreign compulsory-coverage and representative-dispensation gates, not a blanket old exemption/appointment requirement. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:24 | - **Out of scope:** companies, professional traders in securities, property dealers, property held by a company, building land, non-residents (pointer in Cross-border points), crypto-assets (`fr-crypto-tax`). | Exact retained successor LF26, Who this applies to |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:61 | **Surtax brackets** (BOFiP BOI-RFPI-TPVIE-20, PV = taxable gain after the income tax allowance): 50,001 to 60,000: 2% x PV - (60,000 - PV) x 1/20; 60,001 to 100,000: 2% x PV; 100,001 to 110,000: 3% x PV - (110,000 - PV) x 1/10; 110,001 to 150,000: 3% x PV; 150,001 to 160,000: 4% x PV - (160,000 - PV) x 15/100; 160,001 to 200,000: 4% x PV; 200,001 to 210,000: 5% x PV - (210,000 - PV) x 20/100; 210,001 to 250,000: 5% x PV; 250,001 to 260,000: 6% x PV - (260,000 - PV) x 25/100; above 260,000: 6% x PV. | Exact retained successor LF63, Rates, bands and thresholds |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:63 | **When the CSG rise applies.** LFSS 2026 (loi n° 2025-1403), art. 12, raised the CSG on capital income from 9.2% to 10.6% (CSS L. 136-8 I 2°). It applies to income assessed with income tax from the 2025 income year (gains on securities sold in 2025 and declared in 2026 bear 18.6%), and to income taxed at source (dividends, interest) from 1 January 2026. Property gains, rental income, life insurance and home savings stay at 9.2% CSG, so 17.2% in total (L. 136-8 IV). The same rule is in `fr-social-contributions`. | Exact retained successor LF65, Rates, bands and thresholds |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:67 | **Gains on securities.** Shares, company shares (parts sociales) and fund units. Gain = sale price net of fees minus purchase price and fees. Losses of the year are set first against gains of the same year; any unused loss is carried forward against gains of the next ten years (CGI art. 150-0 D, 11; brochure, plus-values). Declared on the 2042 C box 3VG, with form 2074 unless the bank has computed every gain and no allowance or carried-forward loss is used. | Exact retained current LF69, The taxable base Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:69 | - **PFU or scale.** The PFU is the default. The household may opt for the progressive scale by ticking box 2OP (CGI art. 200 A, 2). The option is global: it covers all dividends, interest and gains on securities of the year. For income of 2025 it is irrevocable. For income of 2026 onwards, F2613 says it can be withdrawn within the claim period or during an audit. Crypto has its own option (box 3CN, `fr-crypto-tax`). | Exact retained successor LF71, The taxable base |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:70 | - **What the scale brings.** The 40% dividend allowance, the holding allowances on shares bought before 1 January 2018, the 6.8% deductible CSG, and the deduction of fees. Under the PFU, none of these. F2613: the scale helps at a marginal rate of 0% or 11%; at 30% or more the PFU rate is lower. At 30% with dividends, compute both. Band limits are in `fr-personal-income-tax`. | Exact retained successor LF72, The taxable base |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:71 | - **Holding allowances** apply only if the shares were bought before 1 January 2018 AND the scale is chosen. They cut the income tax base only, never the social levy base or the reference income (impots.gouv.fr, holding allowance on securities: "il n'est pas pris en compte pour les prélèvements sociaux ni pour le revenu fiscal de référence"). | Exact retained successor LF73, The taxable base |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:72 | - **Retiring SME director.** The fixed allowance applies under the PFU or the scale, shares held at least one year, form 2074-DIR. It cannot be combined with the holding allowances. Each spouse or PACS partner taxed jointly gets their own allowance on their own shares, and an unused balance cannot pass to the other (impots.gouv.fr, retiring SME director: "Le reliquat d'abattement non utilisé par l'un des membres du foyer ne peut pas être utilisé par le second"). Social levies are due on the gain before the allowance. Other conditions of art. 150-0 D ter are not set out here: refer. | Exact retained successor LF74, The taxable base |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:74 | **Dividends and interest.** Dividends go in box 2DC (eligible for the allowance under the scale), interest in box 2TR, with no allowance on interest. The bank withholds 12.8% as an advance payment, credited on the return; a household whose reference income of the year before last (revenu fiscal de référence de l'avant-dernière année, F2613) is under the limits may ask to be excused by 30 November of the year before payment. Livret A, livret jeune, LEP and LDD(S) interest is exempt from income tax and social levies. | Exact retained successor LF76, The taxable base |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:76 | **PEA.** Payment ceilings apply to payments in, not to the plan's value. One classic PEA per adult domiciled in France. A withdrawal before the end of the 5th year closes the plan in principle (listed exceptions in F2385) and the net gain is taxed under the PFU or the scale, plus social levies; early withdrawals for death or to fund creating or taking over a business are exempt from income tax only. After 5 years gains are exempt from income tax; social levies are due at each withdrawal at the investment income rate of the year (18.6% in 2026), with special CSG rules for gains or payments from before 2018. Take the split from the bank's IFU form. Exempt income of unlisted shares in a PEA is capped each year at 10% of their value. Only securities the law lists may be held. | Exact retained current LF78, The taxable base Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:78 | **Life insurance (assurance-vie).** Any total or partial withdrawal (rachat) is taxable on the gain it contains, as reported by the insurer on the IFU. The 8-year test runs from the contract date. The yearly allowance covers all contracts together and applies once on the return. The 7.5% rate after 8 years applies up to EUR 150,000 of premiums not repaid across all contracts; the gain linked to premiums above that is taxed at 12.8%. For premiums before 27 September 2017 the final withholding applies only if elected when paid; otherwise the gain goes to the scale. A withdrawal after dismissal, retirement, invalidity or judicial liquidation of the taxpayer or spouse is exempt from income tax, not from social levies. Spreading withdrawals after 8 years within the yearly allowance lowers income tax, not social levies. | Presentation/source table reorganised into Rates, taxable-base, Key law and Sources; substantive values are mapped in their adjacent topic rows. Historical footer wording is replaced by the protected current CTA/disclaimer; no tax rule. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:80 | **Free shares (AGA) and RSUs.** Two amounts, both taxed when the shares are sold: the acquisition gain (value at final vesting) and the later gain on securities. The regime is set by the date of the shareholders' meeting that authorised the plan: the table gives plans from 1 January 2018; plans before 28 September 2012 were taxed at a flat 30% (or the scale on option); plans from 28 September 2012 to 7 August 2015 are salary with the employee contribution; plans from 8 August 2015 to 31 December 2017 have their own rules on the impots.gouv.fr page. The EUR 300,000 limit is yearly across plans, with no carry-forward. A sale below the vesting value gives a loss set against the acquisition gain. Only plans under Commercial Code art. L. 225-197-1 ff. qualify; a foreign RSU plan that does not meet them is taxed as salary: refer. | Exact retained current LF82, The taxable base Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:82 | **BSPCE.** Shares subscribed from 1 January 2025: the exercise gain (value at exercise minus grant price) is taxed at 12.8%, or the salary scale on option, with 3 years or more of service at sale; under 3 years, 30% with no option. The sale gain is under the PFU whatever the service, and the retiring director's allowance can apply. Before 1 January 2025: one gain on sale; granted from 2018 with 3 years or more, PFU or scale; under 3 years, 30% with no option and no fixed allowance; granted to 2017, 19% or 30% by length of service. The company must meet CGI art. 163 bis G. | Exact retained successor LF84, The taxable base |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:84 | **Stock options.** The exercise gain is taxed when the shares are sold and always declared on the 2042 C. From 28 September 2012: salary scale plus the levies and contribution in the table. The 10% employee contribution applies to gains on options granted since 16 October 2007 (impots.gouv.fr, stock options: "Les gains de levée d'options attribués depuis le 16 /10 /2007 sont également soumis à la contribution salariale de 10 %"). 27 April 2000 to 28 September 2012: salary if the lock-up was broken, flat rates if kept, salary on option. 20 September 1995 to 26 April 2000: gain on securities at a flat rate or salary on option. Before 20 September 1995: gain on securities if form and lock-up conditions were met. Options granted before 28 September 2012 bear 18.6% levies. The discount above 5% of the share value at grant is salary in the year of exercise (boxes 1TP or 1UP). The rise after exercise is a separate gain on securities. | Exact retained successor LF86, The taxable base |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:86 | **Employee savings and PER.** Employer payments into an employee savings plan (PEE, PERCO, company PER) are exempt from income tax; their income bears social levies. Match caps follow the annual social security ceiling and are not printed here. Order of use: PEE with match, then PERCO or company PER with match, then individual PER. A PER exit depends on the plan type, annuity or lump sum, timing, and whether payments were deducted: get the provider's split and model it with `fr-personal-income-tax`. | Exact retained current LF88, The taxable base Current FR-R19–26 qualifications govern acquisition basis, loss order/form route, actual unlisted denominator, plan-specific payment and qualifying acquisition timing as applicable; earlier wording is not a universal entitlement. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:88 | **Property gains.** Gain = sale price minus purchase price, the purchase price raised by actual costs or a flat 7.5%, and by actual works or a flat 15% if held over 5 years (F10864). The income tax allowance and the social levy allowance run on different scales (table). Income tax 19% on the gain after the income tax allowance; social levies 17.2% on the gain after the social levy allowance. The surtax applies to the income tax base above EUR 50,000, not to exempt sales or building land. Exemptions: the main home at the time of sale (occupied most of the year), a sale price up to EUR 15,000, and others in F10864 (for example a first sale of another dwelling with the price reinvested in a main home). A non-resident EU or EEA national who was tax resident in France for at least two continuous years gets an exemption on one French dwelling up to EUR 150,000 of net gain, if sold by 31 December of the tenth year after leaving, or at any time if the seller has had free use of it since 1 January of the year before the sale (CGI art. 150 U, II, 2°). The notary computes, files form 2048-IMM-SD and pays the tax out of the sale price; the seller reports the gain the notary declared on the 2042 C. | Presentation/source table reorganised into Rates, taxable-base, Key law and Sources; substantive values are mapped in their adjacent topic rows. Historical footer wording is replaced by the protected current CTA/disclaimer; no tax rule. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:105 | - **Late filing** of the 2042 or 2074: 10% surcharge; 40% if not filed within 30 days of a formal notice; 80% for a hidden activity (CGI art. 1728). | Exact retained successor LF107, Penalties and interest |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:106 | - **Inaccuracy:** 40% for a deliberate breach; 80% for abuse of law or fraudulent manoeuvres (CGI art. 1729). | FR-A09 current penalty paragraph retains fraud80/deliberate40 and adds statutory abuse-of-law reduction40. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:107 | - **Late payment interest:** 0.20% per month (CGI art. 1727), on top of any surcharge. | Exact retained successor LF109, Penalties and interest |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:108 | - **Capital-income CSG assessed with income tax:** 10% surcharge on amounts unpaid 45 days after the assessment (CSS L. 136-6 III; CGI art. 1730). | Exact retained current LF110, Penalties and interest |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:113 | - **Non-residents.** A gain on French property by a non-resident individual is taxed at 19% (CGI art. 244 bis A, I and 200 B), paid when the deed is registered or within one month of the sale, under the responsibility of a representative in France accredited by the tax office (244 bis A, IV). The surtax applies to non-residents too (CGI art. 1609 nonies G, I). Gains on substantial holdings in French companies, social levies and treaty relief: use `fr-cross-border`. | FR-A08 current foreign compulsory-coverage and representative-dispensation gates, not a blanket old exemption/appointment requirement. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:114 | - **Resident insured in another EEA state or Switzerland:** social levies on capital income are the 7.5% solidarity levy only (F2329; CSS L. 136-6 I ter). | FR-A08 current foreign compulsory-coverage and representative-dispensation gates, not a blanket old exemption/appointment requirement. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:115 | - **Foreign dividends and interest:** taxable in France; the foreign tax credit depends on the treaty. Refer with the treaty text. | Exact retained successor LF117, Cross-border points |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5:122 | **Example 1. Shares sold in 2025, flat tax.** A resident single person sold listed shares in 2025 with a net gain of EUR 10,000 and kept the PFU. Income tax 10,000 x 12.8% = 1,280. The disposal is from 2025, so social levies are 18.6%, not 17.2%: 10,000 x 18.6% = 1,860. Total 3,140, declared in May 2026 on the 2042 C box 3VG. | Exact retained successor LF124, Worked examples |

Social levy allowance 7 x 1.65% = 11.55%, base 120,000 x 88.45% = 106,140, levies at 17.2% (property gains stay at 17.2%) = 18,256.08.
